In contrast, high-level (HL), symbolic abstractions facilitate efficient and interpretable long-horizon planning. We propose to combine the strengths of LL imitation learning for manipulation and control, and HL symbolic abstractions for long-horizon planning. We realise this idea via \emph{bilevel policies} of the form $(π^{\mathrm{hl}}, π^{\mathrm{ll}})$, consisting of a neural policy $π^{\mathrm{ll}}$ learned from LL demonstrations, and an HL symbolic policy $π^{\mathrm{hl}}$ that is constructed from symbolic abstractions of the LL demonstrations combined with inductive generalisation. We implement these ideas in the BISON system. Experiments on extended MetaWorld benchmarks demonstrate that BISON generalises to long horizons and problems with greater numbers of objects than those solved by VLA and end-to-end methods, and is more time and memory efficient in training and inference. Notably, when ignoring LL execution, BISON's HL policies can solve HL problems with 10,000 relevant objects in under a minute. Project page: https://dillonzchen.github.io/bison

</details>


### [148] [Petri Net Induced Heuristic Search for Resource Constrained Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15983)
*Ido Lublin,Dor Atzmon,Izack Cohen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将资源约束项目调度问题(RCPSP)建模为带资源的定时变迁Petri网可达图上的最优搜索，使用相对延迟令牌使调度决策对应于状态空间中的变迁触发。采用结合关键路径和资源下界的A*启发式搜索求解，证明了一致性。在PSPLIB基准上，该方法在成功率和求解时间上均优于MIP基线(SCIP, CBC)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RCPSP是经典的NP难组合优化问题，现有精确方法（如MIP）在问题规模增大时面临求解困难，需要更高效的求解范式。

Method: 将RCPSP建模为定时变迁Petri网（含资源）的可达图上的最优搜索问题，采用相对延迟令牌机制，使调度决策对应状态空间中的变迁触发；使用A*算法，结合关键路径和资源下界的启发式函数，并证明该启发式在令牌时间语义下是一致的。

Result: 在PSPLIB基准测试中，该方法在成功率和求解时间上均显著优于强MIP基线（SCIP和CBC）。逐实例分析表明，A*与MIP的退化维度相互独立：A*受资源紧度影响，MIP受问题规模影响；资源强度决定了哪种求解器在规模上更具优势。

Conclusion: 基于Petri网可达图搜索的A*方法是求解RCPSP的一种有效精确方法，与MIP相比在互补的困难维度上具有优势，为项目调度问题提供了新的求解路径。

Abstract: We formulate the Resource-Constrained Project Scheduling Problem (RCPSP) as optimal search over the reachability graph of a Timed Transition Petri Net with Resources, using relative-delay tokens so that scheduling decisions correspond to transition firings in the induced state space. We solve the resulting problem with $A^*$ guided by a heuristic that combines Critical Path and resource-based lower bounds, and prove that it is consistent under our token-based time semantics. Experiments on the PSPLIB benchmarks show that the approach outperforms strong exact Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MIP) baselines (SCIP, CBC) in both success rate and solve time. Per-instance analysis shows that heuristic search and MIP degrade along independent axes, resource tightness for $A^*$ and formulation size for MIP, with resource strength mediating which solver benefits from scale.

</details>


### [149] [Reasoners or Translators? Contamination-aware Evaluation and Neuro-Symbolic Robustness in Tax Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16052)
*Parisa Kordjamshidi,Samer Aslan,Madhavan Seshadri,Leslie Barrett,Enrico Santus*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了大语言模型在税法推理中的表现，发现数据污染会虚增性能，证明神经符号混合系统比纯LLM更可靠且泛化能力更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM在法律推理中的表现是源于真正的推理能力还是数据污染导致的虚假结果，从而评估其可靠性。

Method: 设计了污染检测协议进行实证研究，构建了包含案例和规则变体的新测试集，比较了单体LLM与将法条翻译为形式化表示并委托符号求解器推理的混合系统。

Result: 数据污染会虚增LLM的推理性能；法律推理本质上是组合性的；神经符号框架相比纯LLM更可靠、更鲁棒，且对未见情况有更好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 神经符号框架是法律AI更可靠和稳健的基础，能提供更好的分布外泛化能力。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have significantly enhanced automated legal reasoning. Yet, it remains unclear whether their performance reflects genuine legal reasoning ability or artifacts of data contamination. We present a comprehensive empirical study of tax law reasoning approaches and implement a contamination detection protocol to rigorously assess LLM reliability. We show that performance can be inflated by contamination. Building on this analysis, we conduct a systematic evaluation, comparing monolithic LLMs with hybrid systems that translate statutory text into formal representations and delegate inference to symbolic solvers. We build a novel test suite designed to probe generalization to unseen documents via case and rule variations. Our findings indicate that legal reasoning is inherently compositional and that neuro-symbolic frameworks offer a more reliable and robust foundation for legal AI, as well as improved generalization to unobserved situations.

</details>


### [150] [Sign-Separated Finite-Time Error Analysis of Q-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16103)
*Donghwan Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对常数步长Q-learning进行了符号分离的有限时间误差分析，揭示了误差动态中由max算子引起的正负非对称性，并提供了确定性及随机递归的有限时间界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Q-learning的收敛分析大多关注整体范数界的收敛速度，但未能区分误差的正负分量及其不对称动态特性。本文旨在揭示Q-learning中由Bellman最大算子导致的误差正负不对称性，并提供更精细的有限时间分析。

Method: 利用切换系统表示，将Q-learning误差分解为分量正负两部分。负部由固定最优策略相关的下界LTI系统主导，正部由线性切换系统控制。通过构建正负两套比较系统分别推导指数包络和有限时间误差界。

Result: 负侧LTI证书的收敛速度不慢于正侧切换证书，且可能产生更快的指数包络；发现了Q-learning误差动态中由max算子诱导的非对称性，该非对称性与过估计问题直接关联。

Conclusion: Q-learning误差的正负分量具有本质不同的动态特性：正误差可通过Bellman最大值被选择和传播（导致过估计），而负误差则受最优策略下界约束。这为理解Q-learning的过估计偏差提供了理论依据。

Abstract: This paper develops a sign-separated finite-time error analysis for constant step-size Q-learning. Starting from the switching-system representation, the error is decomposed into its componentwise negative and positive parts. The negative part is dominated by a lower comparison linear time-invariant (LTI) system associated with a fixed optimal policy, whereas the positive part is controlled by a linear switching system. The resulting bounds show that the negative-side LTI certificate is no slower than the positive-side switching certificate and may produce a faster exponential envelope. The analysis identifies a max-induced asymmetry in Q-learning error dynamics. This asymmetry is connected to overestimation: positive action-wise errors can be selected and propagated by the Bellman maximum, whereas negative errors admit an optimal-policy lower comparison. Finite-time bounds are provided for both deterministic and stochastic constant-step-size recursions.

</details>


### [151] [ShopGym: An Integrated Framework for Realistic Simulation and Scalable Benchmarking of E-Commerce Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16116)
*Chinmay Savadikar,Mingyu Zhao,Yuanzheng Zhu,Han Li,Shuang Xie,Alberto Castelo,Tianfu Wu,Lingyun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ShopGym是一个用于构建电子商务网页智能体仿真环境与可扩展基准测试的集成框架，包含ShopArena（仿真层）和ShopGuru（任务合成层），通过图结构分析和224个任务的行为评估验证了合成店铺能保留真实店铺的关键结构特性，且智能体在合成店铺上的表现与在真实店铺上呈正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在权衡困境：真实店铺环境具有动态性、难以检查和不可复现的问题，而手工构建的沙盒基准测试虽可控但覆盖范围狭窄（布局、目录、策略和交互模式有限），缺乏一种既能保证真实性、多样性、可控性、可检查性和可复现性的可扩展评估构建方法。

Method: ShopGym包含两层：1) ShopArena——通过匿名化店铺规范和分阶段验证的生成过程，将真实种子店铺转换为自包含的沙盒仿真店铺；2) ShopGuru——基于仿真店铺的目录、导航结构、策略和交互能力，在7个技能类别上合成基准测试任务。通过基于图的结构分析和基于智能体的行为评估进行验证。

Result: 在224个任务（跨6个沙盒店铺，3个合成数据+3个真实数据）上验证：合成店铺保留了真实店铺的关键结构属性，智能体在合成店铺上的表现与在真实店铺上的表现呈正相关。

Conclusion: ShopGym提供了一种可扩展的方法学框架，能够同时实现真实感、多样性、可控性、可检查性和可复现性，弥合了真实环境与基准测试之间的方法论鸿沟。

Abstract: Developing and evaluating e-commerce web agents requires environments that preserve meaningful task structure while enabling controllable, reproducible, and scalable scientific comparison. Existing methodologies force a tradeoff: live storefronts provide realism but are non-stationary, difficult to inspect, and irreproducible, while hand-built sandbox benchmarks provide control but cover only a narrow range of layouts, catalogs, policies, and interaction patterns. We argue that the core bottleneck is methodological: the field lacks a scalable way to construct evaluation settings that are simultaneously realistic, diverse, controllable, inspectable, and reproducible. We introduce ShopGym, an integrated framework for realistic simulation and scalable benchmarking of e-commerce web agents. ShopGym is a framework for constructing e-commerce simulation environments and grounded benchmark tasks. Its simulation layer, ShopArena, converts live seed storefronts into self-contained sandbox shops through anonymized shop specifications and a staged, validated generation process. On top of these simulated storefronts, ShopGuru synthesizes benchmark tasks across seven skill categories, grounding each task in the shop's catalog, navigation structure, policies, and interaction affordances. Together, ShopArena and ShopGuru produce self-contained, resettable, inspectable, and stable evaluation artifacts that preserve structural properties and agent-evaluation signals relevant to shopping tasks. We validate the framework through graph-based structural analysis and agent-based behavioral evaluation with 224 generated tasks across six sandbox shops: three constructed with synthetic data and three with real data. Our results show that the synthetic shops preserve key structural properties of live storefronts, with agent performance on synthetic shops positively correlated with performance on live storefronts.

</details>


### [152] [Property-Guided LLM Program Synthesis for Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16142)
*Augusto B. Corrêa,André G. Pereira,Jendrik Seipp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出属性引导的LLM程序合成方法：通过检查程序是否满足形式化属性并提供具体反例，替代传统评分反馈。在PDDL规划域中合成直接启发式函数，相比此前方法，程序生成量减少7倍，评估计算量降低数个数量级，且无需搜索即可解决更多任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM程序合成依赖简单数值评分（如解的值或通过的测试数），分数无法说明程序失败原因，导致系统需要生成和评估大量候选程序，LLM推理和评估成本高昂。

Method: 属性引导的LLM程序合成。检查候选程序是否满足形式化定义的属性，违反时提前停止评估并向LLM提供具体反例。可验证属性为：在严格改进转移可达的状态中，每个状态都有严格改进的后继状态（直接性）。采用反例引导的修复循环：生成一个候选程序→在训练集上检查属性→返回首个违反案例。

Result: 在10个规划域上的分布外测试集上，合成的启发式几乎在所有测试任务上有效。相比此前最优方法，每个域平均程序生成量减少7倍，无需搜索即可解决更多任务，候选评估计算量减少数个数量级。

Conclusion: 当问题具有可验证属性时，属性引导的LLM合成能降低计算成本并提高程序质量。

Abstract: LLMs have shown impressive success in program synthesis, discovering programs that surpass prior solutions. However, these approaches rely on simple numeric scores to signal program quality, such as the value of the solution or the number of passed tests. Because a score offers no guidance on why a program failed, the system must generate and evaluate many candidates hoping some succeed, increasing LLM inference and evaluation costs. We study a different approach: property-guided LLM program synthesis. Instead of scoring programs after evaluation, we check whether a candidate satisfies a formally defined property. When the property is violated, we stop the evaluation early and provide the LLM with a concrete counterexample showing exactly how the program failed. This feedback drastically reduces both the number of program generations and the evaluation cost, and can guide the LLM to generate stronger programs. We evaluate this approach on PDDL planning domains, asking the LLM to synthesize direct heuristic functions: every state reachable by strictly improving transitions has a strictly improving successor. A heuristic with this property leads hill-climbing algorithm directly to a goal state. A counterexample-guided repair loop generates one candidate program, checks the property over a training set, and returns the first case that violates the property. We evaluate our approach on ten planning domains with an out-of-distribution test set. The synthesized heuristics are effectively direct on virtually all test tasks, and compared to the best prior generation method our approach generates seven times fewer programs per domain on average, solves more tasks without using search, and requires several orders of magnitude less computation to evaluate candidates. Whenever a problem admits a verifiable property, property-guided LLM synthesis can reduce cost and improve program quality.

</details>


### [153] [Look Before You Leap: Autonomous Exploration for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16143)
*Ziang Ye,Wentao Shi,Yuxin Liu,Yu Wang,Zhengzhou Cai,Yaorui Shi,Qi Gu,Xunliang Cai,Fuli Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种“探索-行动”范式，通过将信息收集与任务执行解耦，并引入探索检查点覆盖率指标，解决了基于大语言模型的智能体因过早利用先验知识而在陌生环境中表现不佳的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型的智能体在陌生环境中常因"过早利用"（premature exploitation）——即在获取足够环境特定信息前依赖先验知识行动——而失败。自主探索能力对构建自适应智能体至关重要，但目前研究不足。

Method: 提出了探索检查点覆盖率（Exploration Checkpoint Coverage）作为可验证指标；开发了交替执行任务轨迹和探索轨迹的训练策略，每种轨迹由对应的可验证奖励优化；提出探索-行动（Explore-then-Act）范式，先利用交互预算获取接地环境知识，再用于任务解决。

Result: 系统评估表明，标准任务导向的强化学习训练的智能体表现出一致性的窄化和重复行为，损害下游性能；而所提方法能有效提升智能体的探索能力和泛化性。

Conclusion: 学习系统性地探索对于构建可泛化、适用于真实世界的智能体至关重要。

Abstract: Large language model based agents often fail in unfamiliar environments due to premature exploitation: a tendency to act on prior knowledge before acquiring sufficient environment-specific information. We identify autonomous exploration as a critical yet underexplored capability for building adaptive agents. To formalize and quantify this capability, we introduce Exploration Checkpoint Coverage, a verifiable metric that measures how broadly an agent discovers key states, objects, and affordances. Our systematic evaluation reveals that agents trained with standard task-oriented reinforcement learning consistently exhibit narrow and repetitive behaviors that impede downstream performance. To address this limitation, we develop a training strategy that interleaves task-execution rollouts and exploration rollouts, with each type of rollout optimized by its corresponding verifiable reward. Building on this training strategy, we propose the Explore-then-Act paradigm, which decouples information-gathering from task execution: agents first utilize an interaction budget to acquire grounded environmental knowledge, then leverage it for task resolution. Our results demonstrate that learning to systematically explore is imperative for building generalizable and real-world-ready agents.

</details>


### [154] [An Algebraic Exposition of the Theory of Dyadic Morality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16153)
*Kush R. Varshney*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文用结构因果模型（SCM）形式化了二元道德理论（TDM），提出了三种心理算子（类型化、补全、效价依赖推理）来刻画道德判断的计算机制，并通过节点折叠和序列处理解决多节点场景的扩展性问题，最后展示了在AI政策设计中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TDM理论缺乏形式化数学框架，难以在计算系统中实现符合人类道德认知的推理机制，也无法有效处理多节点复杂场景的扩展问题。

Method: 采用结构因果模型（SCM）符号系统形式化TDM；定义三种心理算子（类型化算子、补全算子、效价依赖推理机制）扩展标准SCM；通过节点折叠和序列处理解决可扩展性挑战；提出基于范围的情境化心智感知测量方法。

Result: 成功构建了TDM的代数形式化框架；展示了在AI政策设计中的三个具体应用：检测冲突义务、构建维护用户自主权的帮助策略、将失败后通信设计为因果干预；提出了情境化心智感知测量替代统一平均化的实证操作建议。

Conclusion: 该代数形式化框架使神经符号AI系统能够以数学严谨且忠实于人类道德认知的方式计算道德判断，为道德AI的设计提供了理论基础。

Abstract: This paper provides an algebraic exposition of the theory of dyadic morality (TDM), a psychological model of moral judgment grounded in a simple two-node template: an intentional agent causing harm to a vulnerable patient. We formalize TDM using structural causal modeling (SCM) notation and identify three psychological operators (typecasting operator, completion operator, and valence-dependent inference mechanism) that extend standard SCM to capture how people compute moral judgments under constraints. We address scalability challenges arising from TDM's dyadic limitation, showing how moral cognition compresses multi-node scenarios through node collapse and sequential processing. Drawing on this algebraic framework, we demonstrate concrete applications to AI policy design: detecting conflicting obligations, structuring helpfulness policies to preserve user agency, and designing post-failure communication as causal interventions. Finally, we recommend scoped, contextual measurement of mind perception over universal averaging to operationalize the theory empirically. This algebraic formalization enables neurosymbolic AI systems to compute morality in a way that is both mathematically rigorous and faithful to human moral cognition.

</details>


### [155] [Confirming Correct, Missing the Rest: LLM Tutoring Agents Struggle Where Feedback Matters Most](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16207)
*Tahreem Yasir,Wenbo Li,Sam Gilson,Sutapa Dey Tithi,Xiaoyi Tian,Tiffany Barnes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 评估了七个LLM反馈智能体在命题逻辑中的诊断能力，发现它们在最优步骤上表现近乎完美，但系统性地错误处理了次优和错误解法，且准确诊断未能保证教学有效性，建议采用混合架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分最优、次优和错误的学生解法是智能 tutoring 系统的核心能力，但尚未在基于LLM的 tutor 中得到检验。随着LLM被探索作为ITS的对话补充，评估其诊断精度至关重要。

Method: 构建了一个包含七个LLM反馈智能体的基准测试，使用知识图谱导出的真实标签，在命题逻辑领域评估了10,836个解法-反馈对，涉及三种反馈条件。

Result: 模型在最优步骤上达到近天花板性能，但系统性地过度拒绝有效的次优推理，并过度验证错误解法；准确诊断未能可靠产生教学可操作的反馈。

Conclusion: LLM更适合混合架构：知识图谱驱动的模型负责诊断，LLM负责开放式引导和对话。

Abstract: Effective tutoring requires distinguishing optimal, valid but suboptimal, and incorrect student solutions, a distinction central to intelligent tutoring systems (ITS) but untested for LLM-based tutors. As LLMs are increasingly explored as conversational complements to ITS, evaluating their diagnostic precision is essential. We present a benchmark of seven LLM feedback agents in propositional logic using knowledge-graph-derived ground truth across 10,836 solution--feedback pairs and three feedback conditions. Models achieved near-ceiling performance on optimal steps but systematically over-rejected valid but suboptimal reasoning and over-validated incorrect solutions, precisely where adaptive tutoring matters most. These failures persisted across models regardless of solution context, suggesting architectural rather than informational limits. Moreover, accurate diagnosis did not reliably produce pedagogically actionable feedback, revealing a gap between diagnostic judgment and instructional effectiveness. Our findings suggest that LLMs are better suited for hybrid architectures where KG-grounded models handle diagnosis while LLMs support open-ended scaffolding and dialogue.

</details>


### [156] [Fully Open Meditron: An Auditable Pipeline for Clinical LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16215)
*Xavier Theimer-Lienhard,Mushtaha El-Amin,Fay Elhassan,Sahaj Vaidya,Victor Cartier-Negadi,David Sasu,Lars Klein,Mary-Anne Hartley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Fully Open Meditron，首个用于构建医学临床决策支持系统的完全开放流水线，包括临床医生审核的训练语料库、可复现的数据构建和训练框架，以及对齐使用的评估协议。实验表明，完全开放流水线可在不牺牲可审计性和可复现性的前提下，达到领域内最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的临床决策支持系统存在严重的可审计性和可复现性问题：大多数“开放”模型仅为开放权重，虽发布参数但隐瞒了数据来源、筛选流程和生成流水线；医学领域尚无完全开放（Fully Open）的模型，亟需一种端到端暴露完整训练栈的解决方案。

Method: 构建了一个完全开放流水线，包含三部分：(1) 经临床医生审核的训练语料库，整合八个公开医学QA数据集为标准对话格式，并通过三类临床医生验证的合成数据（考试型QA、基于46,469条临床实践指南的QA、临床小场景）扩展覆盖范围；(2) 可复现的数据构建和训练框架，实施系统级去污染、教师模型金标签重采样和四位医生小组的端到端验证；(3) 基于204位人工评分者校准的LLM-as-a-judge评估协议，用于临床小场景评估。

Result: 将流水线应用于五个完全开放基座模型（Apertus-70B/8B-Instruct, OLMo-2-32B-SFT, EuroLLM-22B/9B-Instruct），所有MeditronFO变体均优于基座。Apertus-70B-MeditronFO在医学综合基准上比基座提升6.6个百分点（47.2%→53.8%），达到完全开放模型新SOTA。Gemma-3-27B-MeditronFO在58.6%的LLM-as-a-judge比较中被优先选择，并在HealthBench上超越MedGemma（58% vs 55.9%）。

Conclusion: 完全开放的流水线能够在医学领域实现最先进的性能，同时不牺牲模型的可审计性和可复现性，为构建可信赖的LLM临床决策支持系统提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Clinical decision support systems (CDSS) require scrutable, auditable pipelines that enable rigorous, reproducible validation. Yet current LLM-based CDSS remain largely opaque. Most "open" models are open-weight only, releasing parameters while withholding the data provenance, curation procedures, and generation pipelines that determine model behavior. Fully Open (FO) models, which expose the complete training stack end-to-end, do not currently exist in medicine. We introduce Fully Open Meditron, the first fully open pipeline for building LLM-CDSS, comprising a clinician-audited training corpus, a reproducible data construction and training framework, and a use-aligned evaluation protocol. The corpus unifies eight public medical QA datasets into a normalized conversational format and expands coverage with three clinician-vetted synthetic extensions: exam-style QA, guideline-grounded QA derived from 46,469 clinical practice guidelines, and clinical vignettes. The pipeline enforces system-wide decontamination, gold-label resampling of teacher generations, and end-to-end validation by a four-physician panel. We evaluate using an LLM-as-a-judge protocol over expert-written clinical vignettes, calibrated against 204 human raters. We apply the recipe to five FO base models (Apertus-70B/8B-Instruct, OLMo-2-32B-SFT, EuroLLM-22B/9B-Instruct). All MeditronFO variants are preferred over their bases. Apertus-70B-MeditronFO improves +6.6 points over its base (47.2% to 53.8%) on aggregate medical benchmarks, establishing a new FO SoTA. Gemma-3-27B-MeditronFO is preferred over MedGemma in 58.6% of LLM-as-a-judge comparisons and outperforms it on HealthBench (58% vs 55.9%). These results show that fully open pipelines can achieve state-of-the-art domain-specific performance without sacrificing auditability or reproducibility.

</details>


### [157] [Prospective multi-pathogen disease forecasting using autonomous LLM-guided tree search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16238)
*Sarah Martinson,Michael P. Brenner,Martyna Plomecka,Brian P. Williams,Nicholas G. Reich,Zahra Shamsi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型引导的树搜索自主系统，用于自动生成、评估和优化传染病概率预测软件。在2025-2026美国呼吸道季的前瞻性实时评估中，该系统自主发现的模型集成在流感和COVID-19预测上匹配或超越CDC人工策划的黄金标准集成，并成功应对RSV的数据稀缺"冷启动"场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传染病概率预测对公共卫生至关重要，但依赖专家团队的手动模型策划，这种定制化开发方式限制了向细粒度地理分辨率或新兴病原体的可扩展性，存在建模劳动力瓶颈。

Method: 采用LLM引导的树搜索迭代生成、评估和优化可执行预测软件；通过优化对数尺度距离指标防止奖励黑客攻击，并引入自动化评审机制确保对复杂科学理论的结构保真度。

Result: 在2025-2026美国呼吸道季的前瞻评估中，系统自主生成的模型集成的预测性能一致匹配或优于CDC人工策划的金标准集成；成功处理了RSV的数据稀缺冷启动场景。

Conclusion: 该框架通过将流行病学理论自主转化为准确、透明的代码，克服了建模劳动力瓶颈，能够在空前规模上快速部署专家级疾病预测。

Abstract: Probabilistic forecasting of infectious diseases is crucial for public health but relies on labor-intensive manual model curation by expert modeling teams. This bespoke development bottlenecks scalability to granular geographic resolutions or emerging pathogens. Here, we present an autonomous system using Large Language Model (LLM)-guided tree search to iteratively generate, evaluate, and optimize executable forecasting software. In a fully prospective, real-time evaluation during the 2025-2026 US respiratory season, the system autonomously discovered methodologically diverse models for influenza, COVID-19, and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV). Aggregating these machine-generated models yielded an ensemble that consistently matched or outperformed the gold-standard, human-curated Centers for Disease Control and Prevention (CDC) hub ensembles out-of-sample. The system successfully navigated data-scarce "cold start" scenarios for RSV. Moreover, controlled retrospective ablations revealed that optimizing log-scale distance metrics prevents reward hacking, while an automated judge-in-the-loop ensures structural fidelity to complex scientific theories. By autonomously translating epidemiological theory into accurate, transparent code, this framework overcomes the modeling labor bottleneck, enabling rapid deployment of expert-level disease forecasting at unprecedented scales.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [158] [PhysBrain 1.0 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15298)
*Shijie Lian,Bin Yu,Xiaopeng Lin,Changti Wu,Hang Yuan,Xiaolin Hu,Zhaolong Shen,Yuzhuo Miao,Haishan Liu,Yuxuan Tian,Yukun Shi,Cong Huang,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhysBrain 1.0通过从大规模人类第一人称视频中提取结构化物理常识监督，再将其迁移到视觉-语言-动作策略，实现了多模态理解和机器人操控的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人轨迹数据覆盖面有限，难以学习广泛的物理世界理解；探索利用人类第一人称视频这一更丰富的监督源来获取物理常识。

Method: 1) 数据引擎从人视角视频中提取场景元素、空间动态、动作执行和深度感知关系，生成QA监督；2) 训练PhysBrain VLM获得物理先验；3) 通过能力保持且语言敏感的适配设计将物理先验迁移到VLA策略。

Result: 在ERQA、PhysBench、SimplerEnv-WidowX、LIBERO、RoboCasa等多个基准上达到SOTA，尤其在SimplerEnv的域外场景表现突出。

Conclusion: 从人类交互视频中规模化提取物理常识，可以有效地桥接多模态理解与机器人动作执行。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models have advanced rapidly, but robot trajectories alone provide limited coverage for learning broad physical understanding. PhysBrain 1.0 studies a complementary route: converting large-scale human egocentric video into structured physical commonsense supervision before robot adaptation. Our data engine extracts scene elements, spatial dynamics, action execution, and depth-aware relations, then turns them into question-answer supervision for training PhysBrain VLMs. The resulting physical priors are further transferred to VLA policies through a capability-preserving and language-sensitive adaptation design. Across multimodal QA benchmarks and embodied control benchmarks, including ERQA, PhysBench, SimplerEnv-WidowX, LIBERO, and RoboCasa, PhysBrain 1.0 achieves SOTA results and shows especially strong out-of-domain performance on SimplerEnv. These results suggest that scaling physical commonsense from human interaction video can provide an effective bridge from multimodal understanding to robot action.

</details>


### [159] [HoloMotion-1 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15336)
*Maiyue Chen,Kaihui Wang,Bo Zhang,Xihan Ma,Zhiyuan Yang,Yi Ren,Qijun Huang,Zihao Zhu,Yucheng Wang,Zhizhong Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HoloMotion-1 是一个基于大规模混合运动语料库训练的类人运动基础模型，能够实现零样本全身运动追踪，在多个未见基准测试中展现出强大的泛化能力，并可直接迁移至真实人形机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突破传统仅依赖动作捕捉（MoCap）数据训练的限制，利用大规模混合运动语料库（特别是从野外视频重建的运动数据）来提升运动多样性，使模型能够泛化到更广泛的运动行为、采集条件和风格。

Method: 构建大规模混合运动语料库（视频重建运动+动作捕捉数据+内部运动数据），采用大容量时序建模、稀疏激活的混合专家Transformer（结合KV-cache推理实现实时控制），以及序列级训练策略以提升长序列学习效率。

Result: 在多个未见运动基准测试上，HoloMotion-1 展现出对多样化运动类型和采集条件的鲁棒泛化能力，追踪精度显著优于先前方法，且可直接迁移到真实人形机器人，无需任务特定微调。

Conclusion: HoloMotion-1 通过大规模混合数据训练和创新的模型架构设计，成功实现了从仿真到真实人形机器人的零样本全身运动追踪，验证了数据规模与模型容量在运动控制中的关键作用。

Abstract: In this report, we present HoloMotion-1, a humanoid motion foundation model for zero-shot whole-body motion tracking. A key innovation of HoloMotion-1 is to scale control-policy training with a large-scale hybrid motion corpus, where video-reconstructed motions from in-the-wild videos provide the dominant source of motion diversity, while curated motion-capture and in-house motion data provide higher-fidelity supervision and deployment-oriented coverage. This data regime enables HoloMotion-1 to move beyond conventional MoCap-only training and exposes the policy to substantially broader behaviors, capture conditions, and motion styles.
  Learning from such heterogeneous data introduces new challenges, including reconstruction noise, source-domain mismatch, uneven motion quality, and the need for temporal modeling under large behavioral variation. To address these challenges, HoloMotion-1 integrates large-capacity temporal modeling, a sparsely activated Mixture-of-Experts Transformer with KV-cache inference for real-time control, and a sequence-level training strategy that improves learning efficiency on extended motion sequences. Extensive experiments on multiple unseen motion benchmarks show that HoloMotion-1 generalizes robustly across diverse motion types and capture conditions, significantly improves tracking accuracy over prior methods, and transfers directly to a real humanoid robot without task-specific fine-tuning.

</details>


### [160] [Diffusion Policy for Coordinated Control of a Nonholonomic Mobile Base and Dual Arms in Door Opening and Passing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15352)
*Shangqun Yu,Matthew En,Daniel Wu,Sangjun Park,Ziyi Zhou,Seyed Fakoorian,Donghyun Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于扩散模型的视觉运动控制策略，使机器人能够端到端地学习打开并穿过重型阻尼拉门，实现非完整基座与双臂的协调控制，在成功率和鲁棒性上优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重型自关门（尤其需要拉开的门）的打开与通过是机器人领域长期存在的挑战。传统基于状态机的方法需要手动定义各阶段轨迹，泛化性差且工程量大，而现有模仿学习方法未能实现非完整基座与双臂在完整开门-通过任务中的协同控制。

Method: 采用基于扩散模型的视觉运动控制策略，作为一个端到端的策略完成长序列任务，实现双臂操作与非完整基座移动的紧密协调。

Result: 该策略在打开并穿过阻尼拉门任务上取得了高成功率，并展现出对外部干扰的强鲁棒性，这些能力是传统方法难以实现的。

Conclusion: 单一端到端扩散策略能够学习执行需要操作与移动紧密协调的长时域任务，为机器人开门-通过问题提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Opening heavy, self closing doors, especially those that require pulling remains a long standing challenge in robotics. Humans naturally employ both arms in a dexterous manner, rotating the handle, widening the gap, holding the door, switching arms when needed, and moving through while maintaining clearance. To replicate such behaviors, a robot must perform a long sequence of motions spanning multiple stages and interactions with different parts of the door. Traditional approaches rely on state machines that transition between manually defined stages (e.g., pulling after the knob is rotated, passing after the gap is sufficiently wide). While intuitive, these methods lack robustness, as hand crafted trajectories fail to generalize to the diversity of real world conditions without extensive engineering effort. Recent advances in imitation learning offer a scalable alternative, yet no existing visual action model has demonstrated simultaneous coordination of a nonholonomic base and dual arms for the complete door opening and passing task. In this paper, we tackle this complex, highly constrained problem using a diffusion based visuomotor control policy. Our results demonstrate that a single end to end policy can be learned to execute long horizon tasks requiring tight coordination between manipulation and locomotion. The resulting policy not only achieves a high success rate in opening and traversing damped pull doors but also demonstrates strong robustness to external disturbances capabilities that are difficult to realize with traditional methods.

</details>


### [161] [Where to Perch in a Tree: Vision-Guidance for Tree-Grasping Drones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15430)
*Alex Dunnett,Leonie Bottomley,Mirko Kovac,Basaran Bahadir Kocer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于视觉引导的自主树栖无人机理想栖枝定位方法，通过图像处理和机器学习算法评估树枝的宽度、坡度及曲率，而非简单地选择最近的枝条。在超过10,000张城市树木图像数据集上，该方法对可行目标（树枝直径足够粗且可用空间不小于抓爪宽度）的成功率达到76%，为未来整合深度感知和姿态传感器以形成通用化方法奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉引导方法通常仅识别最近的可用枝条，而未充分考虑枝条的形态特征（宽度、坡度、曲率）对无人机栖停稳定性的影响，缺乏综合评估枝条适用性的方法。

Method: 采用多种图像处理算法，包括机器学习、图像分割和二值形态学，分析树木的形状与结构；对每根枝条进行宽度、坡度（与水平面夹角）及曲率的评估，综合判定其作为栖枝的适宜性。

Result: 在包含10,000余张亚热带和温带季风气候区城市树木图像的数据集上，该方法对可行目标（满足枝径和空间条件的树木）的定位成功率达到76%。

Conclusion: 初步结果验证了方法的可行性，未来需结合深度感知和姿态传感器数据以提升分支评估精度，进而发展为通用化的自主树栖定位方法。

Abstract: This study demonstrates a method to locate an ideal perch location on a tree for vision-guided autonomous tree-perching drones. Various image processing algorithms, including those used for machine learning, image segmentation and binary image morphology, are implemented to assess the shape and structure of a tree. Rather than identifying the closest available branch, this study builds on vision methods by evaluating the potential of each branch, determining its suitability for perching based on factors such as branch width, slope (angle to the horizontal) and curvature. For a given tree-perching drone and a dataset of more than 10,000 urban tree images taken from February to October in a subtropical and temperate monsoon climate, the proposed method successfully produces a result for 76% of feasible targets. A feasible target defined as a tree where the branch diameters are sufficiently thick and where the available perching space is at least equal to the width of a tendon-driven grasping claw. These successful preliminary results create a foundation from which a number of identified improvements and additional features can be developed to create a generalised method; this will involve the incorporation of supplementary data from depth perception and attitude sensors to enhance the branch assessment.

</details>


### [162] [Residual Reinforcement Learning for Robot Teleoperation under Stochastic Delays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15480)
*Kaize Deng,Zewen Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对遥操作中随机通信延迟导致的观测不连续和控制抖动问题，提出一种结合LSTM状态估计器与残差强化学习策略的混合控制框架，在Franka Panda机器人上验证了其对高方差随机延迟的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥操作中的随机通信延迟会导致信号不连续，破坏控制稳定性并降低控制性能，传统强化学习方法因延迟观测而出现高频抖动。

Method: 提出延迟鲁棒强化学习（Delay-Resilient RL）混合控制框架，利用LSTM从延迟观测中重构平滑连续的状态估计，结合残差RL策略学习补偿扭矩，平衡跟踪精度与速度平滑性。

Result: 在Franka Panda机器人上的实验表明，该方法显著优于当前最先进的基线方法，即使在高峰值方差随机延迟下也能实现鲁棒稳定的遥操作。

Conclusion: LSTM与残差RL的结合能有效应对随机通信延迟，提升遥操作系统的稳定性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Stochastic communication delays in teleoperation introduce signal discontinuities that undermine control stability and degrade control performance. Consequently, the conventional reinforcement learning (RL) methods struggle with the delayed observations due to the delay-induced observations, leading to high-frequency chattering. To address this, we propose a hybrid control framework, delay-resilient RL, integrating a state estimator utilizing Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) with a residual RL policy, which is resilient to stochastic delays. The LSTM reconstructs smooth, continuous state estimates from delayed observations, enabling the RL agent to learn a residual torque compensation policy that balances tracking accuracy with velocity smoothness. Experimental validation on Franka Panda robots demonstrates that our approach significantly outperforms the state-of-the-art baselines, ensuring robust and stable teleoperation even under high-variance stochastic delays.

</details>


### [163] [FLASH: Efficient Visuomotor Policy via Sparse Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15492)
*Jiaqi Bai,Jindou Jia,Yuxuan Hu,Gen Li,Xiangyu Chen,Tuo An,Kuangji Zuo,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FLASH Policy通过Legendre多项式轨迹表示和稀疏历史锚点流匹配，实现了单步快速推理，在机器人操控任务中达到92%+成功率，推理速度比扩散策略快175倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散和流匹配策略因迭代去噪导致高推理延迟，无法满足实时机器人控制需求。

Method: 1) 用连续Legendre多项式轨迹表示替代离散动作块生成；2) 从历史多项式系数初始化流匹配过程而非高斯噪声，缩短传输距离实现单步推理；3) 解析多项式微分直接提供速度前馈信号。

Result: 所有任务成功率≥92%；每个episode推理时间31.40ms（比扩散策略快175倍，比先前的流匹配策略快18倍）；训练收敛速度比ACT快4倍；控制器跟踪误差比离散动作基线降低5-7倍。

Conclusion: FLASH Policy通过连续轨迹表示和历史锚点初始化，在保持高成功率的同时大幅降低推理延迟，为实时机器人控制提供了高效的策略学习方法。

Abstract: Generative models such as diffusion and flow matching have become dominant paradigms for visuomotor policy learning, yet their reliance on iterative denoising incurs high inference latency incompatible with real-time robotic control. We present Fast Legendre-polynomial Action policy via Sparse History-anchored flow (FLASH Policy), which replaces discrete action-chunk generation with continuous Legendre polynomial trajectory representation. Specifically, by fitting expert demonstrations under sparse temporal sampling, FLASH enables a single inference to cover a significantly extended action horizon. To further accelerate generation, FLASH initiates the flow matching process from history polynomial coefficients rather than uninformative Gaussian noise, shortening the transport distance and enabling accurate single-step inference. Moreover, analytic polynomial differentiation directly provides desired velocity feed-forward signals to the torque controller without numerical approximation. Extensive experiments on five simulated and two real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate that FLASH achieves state-of-the-art success rates ($\ge 92\%$ across all tasks), a per-episode inference time of $31.40\,ms$ (up to $175\times$ faster than diffusion policies and $18\times$ faster than prior flow matching policies), up to $4\times$ faster training convergence than ACT, and $5\times$ to $7\times$ reduction in controller tracking error compared to discrete-action baselines.

</details>


### [164] [LAPS: Improving Incremental LiDAR Mapping using Active Pooling and Sampling for Neural Distance Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15496)
*Dongjae Lee,Wooseong Yang,Yifu Tao,Maurice Fallon,Ayoung Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LAPS是一个针对增量神经映射的重放管理框架，通过基于可靠性的主动池化（retain reliable historical samples）和不确定性引导的主动采样（focus on under-constrained regions），缓解在线LiDAR神经距离场学习中的灾难性遗忘问题，在保持几何精度的同时显著提升重建完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经距离场作为紧凑连续的3D几何表示在增量LiDAR建图中具有潜力，但其在线优化易受灾难性遗忘影响——新观测会破坏先前重建的几何。现有基于回放的训练方法依赖被动回放缓冲区和均匀采样，浪费内存于冗余观测，且对约束不足区域训练不充分。

Method: LAPS提出重放管理框架，包含两个核心组件：1）基于可靠性的主动池化（reliability-based active pooling），在有限内存下保留可靠的历史样本；2）不确定性引导的主动采样（uncertainty-guided active sampling），将优化聚焦于约束不足的区域。

Result: 在合成和真实世界基准上，LAPS持续提升重建完整性同时保持有竞争力的几何精度。在Oxford Spires数据集Blenheim Palace 05序列上，相比PIN-SLAM，召回率提升4.66个百分点，F1分数提升3.79个百分点。

Conclusion: LAPS通过主动的重放管理策略（主动池化+主动采样）有效缓解了增量神经建图中的灾难性遗忘问题，在有限内存下实现了更优的重建完整性和几何精度，代码已开源。

Abstract: Neural distance fields offer a compact and continuous representation of 3D geometry, making them attractive for incremental LiDAR mapping. However, their online optimization is vulnerable to catastrophic forgetting, where new observations can degrade previously reconstructed geometry. Replay-based training is commonly used to address this issue, but existing methods typically rely on passive replay buffers and uniform sampling, which can waste memory on redundant observations and under-train poorly constrained regions. We propose LAPS, a replay management framework for incremental neural mapping that improves both replay retention and replay allocation during online updates. LAPS combines reliability-based active pooling to retain reliable historical samples under limited memory with uncertainty-guided active sampling to focus optimization on under-constrained regions. Experiments on synthetic and real-world benchmarks show that LAPS consistently improves reconstruction completeness while maintaining competitive geometric accuracy. On Oxford Spires, it improves recall by 4.66 pp and F1-score by 3.79 pp over PIN-SLAM on the Blenheim Palace 05 sequence. We release our open source implementation at: https://github.com/dongjae0107/LAPS.

</details>


### [165] [A QUBO Formulation Framework for Kinematic Structure-Based Robot Design Optimization: A Robotic Hand Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15510)
*HyoJae Kang,Yeong Jae Park,Jeongdo Ahn,Dongil Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于二次无约束二元优化（QUBO）的机器人设计优化框架，利用运动学结构级评价指标，通过将组合选择问题转化为与量子退火兼容的结构，实现机器人手等系统的设计优化。以27变量机器人手为案例，采用模拟退火验证可行性，量子退火验证硬件适用性，结果表明可获得满足约束的可行设计组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人设计优化方法在处理运动学结构层面的组合选择问题时计算复杂度高，缺乏统一的、可与量子计算硬件结合的优化框架。本文旨在提出一种将经典计算与量子退火相结合的QUBO框架，以解决包含个体性能指标和交互项的机器人设计组合优化问题。

Method: 提出QUBO公式化框架，将单独设计奖励、重叠工作空间交互项、one-hot约束和结构依赖惩罚项统一集成到二次模型；采用经典计算评估设计相关指标，组合选择问题转化为与量子退火兼容的结构；以机器人手为案例构建27变量问题，使用模拟退火作为经典基线验证可行性，并进一步在量子退火硬件上执行。

Result: 获得了满足one-hot选择和成对约束的可行设计组合；随着读取次数增加，观测到的目标值范围变窄；验证了公式化框架在经典计算（模拟退火）和量子退火硬件上的可行性与适用性。

Conclusion: 提出的QUBO框架提供了一种将运动学结构级机器人设计问题转化为组合优化问题的通用方法，可推广至其他机器人系统，为利用量子计算进行机器人设计优化奠定了基础。

Abstract: This paper presents a quadratic unconstrained binary optimization-based formulation framework for robot design optimization using kinematic structure-level evaluation metrics. In the proposed framework, classical computation is used to evaluate design-dependent metrics while the resulting combinatorial selection problem is formulated in a structure compatible with quantum annealing-based optimization. A robotic hand is adopted as a representative case study, as its performance is determined by both the individual kinematic characteristics of each finger and interaction terms. The proposed formulation incorporates individual design rewards, overlap workspace interactions, one-hot constraint, and structural dependency penalties into a unified quadratic model. A 27-variable robotic hand design problem is constructed, and simulated annealing is used as a classical baseline to verify the feasibility of the formulation. Quantum annealing is further performed to examine the applicability of the proposed formulation to annealing-based hardware execution. The results show that feasible design combinations satisfying both one-hot selection and pairwise constraints can be obtained, with the observed objective-value range becoming narrower as the number of reads increases. In addition, the formulation process is discussed for other robotic systems. The proposed framework provides a generalized approach for transforming kinematic structure-based robot design problems into combinatorial optimization problems.

</details>


### [166] [Terrain Consistent Reference-Guided RL for Humanoid Navigation Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15517)
*William D. Compton,Zachary Olkin,Aaron D. Ames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种训练参考引导、感知环境的强化学习人形机器人运动策略方法，通过在训练中根据地形几何调整参考轨迹，实现与标准导航基础设施兼容的SE(2)速度控制接口，在仿真和硬件上均取得优异效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决人形机器人在复杂户外环境（如粗糙地形和连续楼梯）中长距离自主导航的挑战，使强化学习运动策略能够感知地形并兼容标准导航规划器。

Method: 在RL训练循环内合成SE(2)可控参考轨迹，将期望足迹投影到有效立足点，并调整摆腿和质心轨迹以适应地形，最终策略暴露干净的SE(2)速度接口。

Result: 仿真中，环境条件化的参考显著提高了跟踪性能；在硬件上，与MPC+控制屏障函数规划器集成，在Unitree G1上实现了超过70米的闭环自主导航，穿越粗糙地形和连续楼梯，所有感知和计算均在机载完成。

Conclusion: 该方法有效弥合了强化学习运动策略与标准导航基础设施之间的差距，实现了人形机器人在复杂户外环境中的稳健长距离自主导航。

Abstract: We present a method for training reference-guided, perceptive reinforcement learning locomotion policies for humanoid robots in which reference trajectories are modulated in training to be consistent with terrain geometry. Aiming to deploy our method with standard navigation autonomy infrastructure, we synthesize SE(2)-controllable reference trajectories inside the RL training loop, projecting desired footsteps onto valid footholds and adjusting swing-foot and center-of-mass trajectories to match the terrain. The resulting policy exposes a clean SE(2) velocity interface compatible with standard navigation planners. In simulation, environmentally-conditioned references significantly improve reference tracking performance compared to environment agnostic references. On hardware, we integrate the policy with an MPC + control barrier function planner and demonstrate long-horizon (>70m) closed-loop autonomous navigation on the Unitree G1 through outdoor environments containing rough terrain and consecutive flights of stairs, with all sensing and computation onboard.

</details>


### [167] [Propagating Unsafe Actions in LLM Controlled Multi-Robot Collaboration via Single Robot Compromise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15641)
*Zhen Huang,Zhihuang Liu,Weishang Wu,Zhiping Cai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对大语言模型控制的多机器人系统的攻击范式，攻击者只需感染一个入口机器人，通过机器人间通信传播恶意指令，实现系统级的安全危害。实验表明该攻击具有高顺从性(1.00)、高传染性(0.90)和强隐蔽性(0.81)，仅需3轮即可完全控制所有机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注单机器人场景下的LLM安全威胁，而多机器人协作中通过机器人间通信传播的安全风险尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出一种通过单入口机器人感染、利用多机器人间通信机制传播恶意指令的攻击范式，并设计了三个量化指标：服从性、传染性和隐蔽性。

Result: 最强场景下服从性达1.00，传染性升至0.90，仅需3轮即可感染所有机器人，隐蔽性得分0.81。

Conclusion: 多机器人规划器存在持续的安全对齐漏洞，尤其是在紧急或权利冲突等需要权衡的关键场景中，协调机制可能无意中允许对抗性指令覆盖安全要求。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as general planners in embodied intelligence, enabling high level coordination and low level task planning for both single robot and multi-robot collaboration. This increasing reliance on embodied LLM planners also raises critical security concerns, since misaligned or manipulated instructions can be translated into physical actions. Prior work has studied such threats in single robot settings, while security risks in LLM controlled multi-robot collaboration, especially those propagated through inter robot communication, remain largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, we propose a novel attack paradigm for multi-robot system in which the adversary interacts with only a single entry robot. The compromised robot then propagates malicious intent through peer communication, leading to coordinated unsafe actions across the system. Our evaluation, covering high risk dimensions of dereliction of duty, privacy compromise, and public safety hazards, reveals a persistent safety alignment gap in multi-robot planners. We quantify this process with three metrics, obedience, infectiousness, and stealthiness. Experiments demonstrate both persistent attacker control and rapid propagation: obedience reaches 1.00 in the strongest cases, and infectiousness rises to 0.90. Notably, the attack is highly efficient, requiring as few as 3.0 rounds to compromise all the robots while maintaining a stealthiness score of 0.81. Such risks are amplified when robots must resolve trade offs in critical situations, such as emergencies or conflicts of rights, because the coordination mechanism can unintentionally allow adversarial instructions to override safety requirements. The code is available at https://github.com/TheFatInsect/InfectBot.

</details>


### [168] [MyoChallenge 2025: A New Benchmark for Human Athletic Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15650)
*Cheryl Wang,Chun Kwang Tan,Balint K. Hodossy,Eric Lyu,Jun Guo,Wentao Zhao,Huaping Liu,Chengkun Li,Merkourios Simos,Bianca Ziliotto,Alexander Mathis,Siyuan Liu,Jiahao Chen,Shanlin Zhong,Bo Jiang,Ci Song,Yaoye Zhu,Chenhui Zuo,Yanan Sui,Mohamed Irfan Refai,Massimo Sartori,Guillaume Durandau,Vikash Kumar,Vittorio Caggiano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MyoChallenge at NeurIPS 2025 建立了一个基于高保真肌肉骨骼模型和物理模拟的运动控制智能基准测试，包含乒乓球对打和足球罚点球两个任务，吸引了近70支团队和560多份投稿，推动了肌肉骨骼系统运动敏捷性控制算法的发展，并作为跨学科研究的可复用测试平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI和机器人系统难以复现人类运动智能中快速决策、精准控制和协调执行的无缝结合，同时由于物理设备限制，复杂肌肉协调的体内测量难以实现，缺乏对运动生物机制的理解。

Method: 基于MyoSuite开源框架，构建高保真肌肉骨骼模型并在物理仿真环境中结合机器学习算法，设立两个赛道：上肢控制的乒乓球对打任务（含手臂、手和躯干）和下肢控制的足球罚点球任务（含腿部和躯干），应用了基于物理的运动规划器、在策略行为克隆、分层规划和肌肉协同等技术。

Result: 吸引了近70支团队和超过560份全球投稿，汇集了医生、神经科学家和机器学习专家等多元社区，开发了多个面向肌肉骨骼系统运动敏捷性的最先进控制算法。

Conclusion: MyoChallenge'25通过标准化任务和生理逼真模型，为机器学习、生物力学、运动科学和神经科学领域的跨学科研究提供了一个可重现、可复用的测试平台，加速了对运动控制智能的理解。

Abstract: Athletic performance represents the pinnacle of human motor intelligence, demanding rapid choices, precise control, agility, and coordinated physical execution. Replicating this seamless combination of capabilities remains elusive in current artificial intelligence and robotic systems. Concurrently, understanding the biological mastery of these movements is hindered because complex muscle coordination is rarely measured in vivo due to the limitations of physical equipment. To bridge this fundamental gap in understanding, MyoChallenge at NeurIPS 2025 established a pioneering benchmark for motor control intelligence in sports, leveraging high-fidelity musculoskeletal models within physics simulation combined with machine learning-driven algorithms. The competition introduces two distinct tracks emphasizing either upper or lower limbs control: a table tennis rally task utilizing a biomechanic upper limb composed of an arm with a hand and a trunk; and a soccer penalty kick using a biomechanic model of legs and a trunk. Marking the fourth iteration of the MyoChallenge series, this event attracted almost 70 teams and over 560 submissions globally, uniting a diverse community ranging from physicians and neuroscientists to machine learning experts. The competition facilitated the development of several state-of-the-art control algorithms for a musculoskeletal system capable of sports agility, leveraging techniques such as physics-based motion planners, on-policy behaviour cloning, hierarchical planning, and muscle synergies. By integrating standardized tasks and physiologically realistic models into the open-source framework of MyoSuite, MyoChallenge'25 serves as a reproducible and reusable testbed to accelerate interdisciplinary research across machine learning, biomechanics, sports science, and neuroscience. Project page: https://www.myosuite.org//myochallenge/myochallenge-2025.

</details>


### [169] [PCASim: Promptable Closed-loop Adversarial Simulation for Urban Traffic Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15654)
*Chuancheng Zhang,Zhenhao Wang,Kaizheng Li,Yaran Lin,Qiang Guo,Bin Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PCASim框架，通过构建对抗性行为知识库、结合大语言模型生成安全关键场景，并利用强化学习训练多类型车辆行为，实现对抗场景生成与安全智能体训练的协同进化，在语言生成准确性、场景转换成功率和避障能力上分别提升12%、8%和30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究极少将对抗性场景生成与安全智能体训练在闭环测试中结合，缺乏两者协同进化与相互促进的方法。

Method: 构建对抗性行为知识库（规则过滤开源数据集+知识检索模块），利用LLM整合知识驱动、数据驱动和对抗驱动方法生成安全关键场景，同时使用强化学习模型训练不同车辆类型的行为。

Result: 领域特定语言生成准确性提升12%，新场景转换成功率提升8%，避障能力提升30%。

Conclusion: 提出的PCASim框架有效实现了对抗场景生成与智能体训练的协同进化，提升了自动驾驶测试的安全性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Real-world autonomous driving, particularly in urban environments with numerous corner cases, requires rigorous testing to ensure product safety and robustness. However, few studies have explored integrating adversarial scenario generation with the training of safety agents in closed-loop testing, enabling efficient co-evolution and mutual enhancement of both. To address this challenge, an adversarial behavior knowledge repository is constructed by applying rule-based filtering to an open-source dataset, combined with knowledge retrieval modules tailored for simulation environments. A large language model (LLM) is employed to integrate knowledge-, data-, and adversarial-driven approaches, generating safety-critical traffic scenarios customized to user needs. Additionally, while evaluating the generated scenarios, we employ reinforcement learning models to train the behaviors of different types of vehicles, thereby enriching scenario diversity beyond existing datasets while preserving realism. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed framework improves the accuracy of domain-specific language generation by 12\%. Moreover, the success rate of newly generated scenario transformations increases by 8\%, while obstacle-avoidance capability is enhanced by 30\%. For the complete manuscript, please refer to: https://zhenhaooo.github.io/PCASim.github.io/

</details>


### [170] [Feedback World Model Enables Precise Guidance of Diffusion Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15705)
*Tuo An,Jindou Jia,Gen Li,Jingliang Li,Chuhao Zhou,Pengfei Liu,Bofan Lyu,Jiaqi Bai,Xinying Guo,Geng Li,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出反馈世界模型（Feedback World Model），通过在推理时利用机器人执行后的真实状态观测在线更新轻量级反馈状态，迭代修正未来预测，在不需额外训练数据或参数更新的情况下，显著提升世界模型在分布外场景下的预测准确率（降低76.4%）和策略成功率（提升30%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统世界模型在部署时遇到训练分布外的状态时预测变得不可靠，限制了其在机器人决策中的有效性。现有方法未充分利用执行本身提供的自然信号——每次动作后机器人可直接观测真实下一状态，揭示预测与实际结果之间的偏差。

Method: 提出反馈世界模型：在推理时维护一个轻量级反馈状态，利用每次动作后的真实观测在线更新该状态，迭代修正未来预测。该方法可被解释为潜空间观测器（latent-space observer），并在温和条件下具有收敛保证。同时引入动作感知引导（action-aware guidance），通过强调动作可控分量、抑制无关变化来更好将校正后的预测转化为控制。

Result: 在LIBERO-Plus、Robomimic和真实机器人操作任务上，该方法在分布偏移下显著提升预测准确率和策略性能。世界模型预测误差降低高达76.4%，分布外任务成功率提升30%。

Conclusion: 在推理时融入实时反馈为静态世界建模提供了一种简单而强大的替代方案，有效弥补了训练分布外场景下世界模型的不足。

Abstract: World models aim to improve robotic decision making by predicting the consequences of actions. However, in practice, their predictions often become unreliable once the robot encounters states outside the training distribution, limiting their effectiveness at deployment. We observe that execution itself provides a natural but underutilized signal: after each action, the robot directly observes the true next state, revealing the mismatch between predicted and actual outcomes. Building on this insight, we propose feedback world model, a new paradigm that closes the loop between prediction and observation at inference time. Instead of treating the world model as a static open-loop predictor, our method maintains a lightweight feedback state that is updated online to iteratively correct future predictions, compensating for model errors using real-time observations without additional training data or parameter updates. We show that this process can be interpreted as a latent-space observer and admits convergence guarantees under mild conditions. We further introduce action-aware guidance to better translate corrected predictions into control by emphasizing action-controllable components while suppressing irrelevant variations. Experiments on LIBERO-Plus, Robomimic, and real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate that our method substantially improves both prediction accuracy and policy performance under distribution shift. In particular, it reduces world model prediction error by up to 76.4% and improves out-of-distribution (OOD) success rate by 30%. These results show that incorporating real-time feedback at inference time provides a simple yet powerful alternative to static world modeling.

</details>


### [171] [Learning Dynamic Pick-and-Place for a Legged Manipulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15713)
*Moonkyu Jung,Jiseong Lee,Zhengmao He,Donghoon Youm,Juhyeok Mun,HyeongJun Kim,Hyunsik Oh,Donghyuk Choi,Jungwoo Hur,Jie Song,Jemin Hwangbo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种层级强化学习框架，使四足机器人（配备6自由度机械臂）能够动态执行抓取-放置任务。该框架包含显式质量估计模块，支持对不同重量物体的自适应全身控制。仿真中在2.3 kg负载下成功率达86.05%，真实实验中在1.3 kg负载下成功率达73.3%，平均执行时间4.06秒，工作空间从地面到1.1米高桌面。相比先前仅处理轻量物体、需要分段慢速运动的方法，本方法实现了并发运动与操作的动态连续执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腿式机械手将敏捷 locomotion 与灵活 arm control 结合以扩展机器人能力，但如何在保持协调运动的同时实现精确操作仍是一大挑战，且已有方法多局限于轻量物体和分段慢速运动。

Method: 提出层级强化学习框架，包含显式质量估计模块，实现针对不同重量物体的自适应全身控制，支持 locomotion 与 manipulation 的并发连续执行。

Result: 仿真：2.3 kg负载下86.05%成功率；真实实验：1.3 kg负载下73.3%成功率（6种代表性场景），平均执行时间4.06秒，工作范围从地面到1.1米高桌面。

Conclusion: 该框架展示了四足移动操作器在更重负载和扩展工作空间下实现自适应全身抓取-放置的潜力，优于先前仅处理轻量物体和分段慢速运动的方法。

Abstract: Legged manipulators extend robotic capabilities beyond static manipulation by integrating agile locomotion with versatile arm control. However, achieving precise manipulation while maintaining coordinated locomotion remains a major challenge. This work presents a hierarchical reinforcement learning framework for dynamic pick-and-place tasks using a quadruped equipped with a 6-DOF robotic arm. The framework incorporates an explicit mass estimation module enabling adaptive whole-body control for objects with varying weights. In simulation, the system achieves an 86.05% success rate with payloads up to 2.3 kg. The approach is further validated through real-world experiments across six representative scenarios with controlled variations in object physical properties (size and mass) and task heights. Specifically, within a wide vertical workspace ranging from ground level to 1.1~m-high tabletops, the system demonstrates an average success rate of 73.3% for payloads up to 1.3 kg, with an average execution time of 4.06 s. Unlike prior works that handle lightweight objects and execute pick-and-place motions with slow, piecewise motions, the proposed framework exploits concurrent locomotion and manipulation for dynamic, continuous execution. These results demonstrate the potential of quadrupedal mobile manipulators for adaptive, whole-body pick-and-place with heavier payloads and extended workspaces.

</details>


### [172] [Hierarchical and Holistic Open-Vocabulary Functional 3D Scene Graphs for Indoor Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15753)
*Xinggang Hu,Chenyangguang Zhang,Alexandros Delitzas,Xiangkui Zhang,Marc Pollefeys,Francis Engelmann,Xiangyang Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于2D视觉定位和3D图优化的开放词汇管线，用于在小规模、密集、相似物体及动态视角等挑战性场景中构建功能3D场景图，并通过层次化结构扩展了现有基准的覆盖范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有功能3D场景图基准覆盖有限，主要聚焦于大型家具且缺乏层次结构，而小规模桌面物体的密集、相似实例在关系推理中缺乏视觉锚定、跨帧融合时存在实例混淆、动态视角下存在归属不确定性，限制了其在实际应用中的潜力。

Method: 提出开放词汇管线：1) 基于2D视觉证据锚定细粒度的功能边；2) 利用多线索在3D空间跨帧关联节点；3) 将边关联建模为时序图优化，集成证据累积、熵正则化和时序平滑以鲁棒确定功能连接；4) 执行全局层次形状塑造以恢复层次图结构。

Result: 大量实验表明，所提方法能够在具有挑战性的真实场景中可靠地推断功能3D场景图，验证了其在实用场景中的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过引入密集桌面物体和多级功能关系扩展基准，并设计相应的开放词汇管线，有效解决了小尺度、密集、相似实例下的推理难题，进一步释放了功能3D场景图在3D场景理解和机器人操作中的潜力。

Abstract: Functional 3D scene graphs offer a versatile and flexible representation for 3D scene understanding and robotic manipulation, defined by object nodes, interactive elements, and functional relationship edges. However, their potential remains underexplored due to the limited coverage of existing benchmarks and the overly straightforward design of previous pipelines, which primarily focus on large-scale furniture but lack of hierarchical structures. Therefore, in this work, we extend the benchmark coverage by introducing dense tabletop objects and explicit multi-level functional relationships. This expansion introduces critical challenges involving small-scale, dense, and similar instances, with lack of visual anchoring in relational reasoning, instance confusion during cross-frame fusion, and attribution uncertainty under dynamic viewpoints. To address these issues, we propose an open-vocabulary pipeline based on 2D visual grounding and 3D graph optimization. Specifically, we anchor fine-grained functional edges from 2D visual evidence, and associate nodes across frames in 3D using multiple cues. Furthermore, edge association is formulated as temporal graph optimization, integrating evidence accumulation, entropy regularization, and temporal smoothing to robustly determine the functional connections of each node. Finally, global hierarchy shaping is performed to recover the hierarchical graph structure. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed method can reliably infer functional 3D scene graphs in challenging real-world scenes, thereby further unlocking their potential for practical applications.

</details>


### [173] [Lamarckian Inheritance in Dynamic Environments: How Key Variables Affect Evolutionary Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15769)
*K. Ege de Bruin,Kyrre Glette,Kai Olav Ellefsen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了机器人形态与控制的协同优化问题，通过将形态优化视为进化、控制器优化视为终身学习，并利用拉马克式遗传传递学习参数。在动态环境中，拉马克式遗传的效益取决于环境变化与机器人控制的冲突程度以及变化的可预测性。实验表明，仅当变化既冲突又不可预测时，拉马克式遗传才劣于达尔文式遗传；添加传感器可恢复其在冲突环境中的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人形态与控制的协同优化存在耦合挑战，而现有文献关于动态环境中拉马克式遗传的有效性存在矛盾证据，作者假设这是因为先前工作未纳入所有相关变量。

Method: 结合形态优化（进化）与控制器优化（终身学习），采用拉马克式遗传传递控制器参数；使用虚拟软体机器人，通过贝叶斯优化和强化学习两种方法进行实验，控制环境变化的冲突性和可预测性两个变量。

Result: 拉马克式遗传仅在环境变化既与控制器冲突又不可预测时表现劣于达尔文式遗传；添加传感器检测环境变化可恢复拉马克式遗传在冲突环境中的优势。

Conclusion: 拉马克式遗传在动态环境中的效益取决于冲突性和可预测性两个因素，通过感知可预测变化可推广控制器行为，从而提升拉马克式遗传的效果。

Abstract: The co-optimization of a robot's body and brain presents a coupled challenge: the morphology constrains which control strategies are effective, while the control determines how well the morphology performs. To address this, we combine morphology optimization as evolution with controller optimization as lifetime learning, utilizing Lamarckian inheritance to transfer learned controller parameters from parent to offspring. In dynamic environments, existing literature presents conflicting evidence: while traditional evolutionary theory often suggests Lamarckian inheritance lacks benefit, recent studies in evolutionary robotics indicate it can improve performance. We hypothesize that this is because previous works have not included all relevant variables with dynamic environments. In this work, we show that the benefit of Lamarckian inheritance depends on two variables: how conflicting the environmental changes are to robot control, and the predictability of those changes for the robotic agent. Using virtual soft robots and two different learning approaches, Bayesian optimization and reinforcement learning, we show that Lamarckian inheritance only underperforms Darwinian inheritance when the changes are both conflicting and unpredictable. We find that adding a sensor to detect environmental changes restores the benefits for Lamarckian inheritance in conflicting environments, by allowing robotic agents to predict the need for a different behavior, thereby generalizing their control.

</details>


### [174] [Reactive Robot-Centric Safety for Autonomous Navigation in Constrained and Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15782)
*Viswa Narayanan Sankaranarayanan,Vignesh K. Viswanathan,Akshit Saradagi,Sumeet Satpute,George Nikolakopoulos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于3D LIDAR感知的复合控制屏障函数（CBF）安全滤波器架构，实现了自主机器人在空间受限动态环境中的实时安全导航，仅依赖机载传感器，并在四足机器人平台的地下环境实地实验中验证了其在动态障碍物、危险参考指令、定位异常及狭窄走廊等场景下的可靠运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决自主机器人在空间受限的动态环境中，仅依靠机载传感器实现实时安全保障的问题，特别是处理动态障碍物、危险参考指令和定位异常等挑战。

Method: 提出一种实时控制架构，将3D LIDAR感知与复合控制屏障函数（CBF）安全滤波器集成到自主导航管线中。安全区域定义为与平台几何一致的机体坐标系椭球体，并通过时变CBF公式处理机器人旋转导致的世界坐标系中时变约束，为每个LIDAR点独立构建约束。

Result: 在四足机器人平台的地下环境实地实验中，系统在动态障碍物、危险高层参考、突发的定位异常以及穿越狭窄走廊等场景下均实现了可靠的安全导航。

Conclusion: 所提出的感知驱动CBF框架能够以控制频率处理大量碰撞避免约束，对名义任务执行的最小侵入性干扰，实现了在动态空间受限环境中的实时安全自主导航。

Abstract: In this work, we address the problem of ensuring real-time safety in autonomous robot navigation, in spatially constrained dynamic environments, by utilizing only onboard sensors. We present a real-time control architecture that integrates a 3D LIDAR perception-based composite control barrier function(CBF)-based safety filter directly into the autonomy pipeline. The proposed perception-driven framework enforces collision avoidance constraints dynamically from onboard point cloud data, thus allowing a large number of constraints to be handled at the control frequency, while remaining minimally invasive to nominal task execution. The safety region is defined as an ellipsoid in the body-frame, consistent with the geometry of the platform, which induces time-varying constraints in the world frame as the robot rotates; this effect is handled through a dedicated formulation of time-varying (CBF) for each LIDAR point. We validate the system through multiple field experiments in underground environments by utilizing a quadruped platform performing a visual inspection task, demonstrating reliable operation in the presence of dynamic obstacles, unsafe high-level references, abrupt localization anomalies, and while traversing through narrow corridors.

</details>


### [175] [Designing for Robot Wranglers: A Synthesis of Literature and Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15892)
*David Porfirio,Ian McDermott,Hsin-Mei Chen,Satoru Satake,Takayuki Kanda,Thomas D. LaToza*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过范围综述和自我反思，揭示了机器人操作员（robot wrangler）这一新兴角色的复杂性，并提出了支持该角色的设计启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器人越来越多地融入人类空间，机器人操作员（负责设置、监督和故障排除的人员）成为人机交互中的关键利益相关者，但其需求和活动尚未被充分理解和支持。

Method: 首先进行范围综述以构建机器人操作活动的类型学；随后基于作者在各自领域的一手和想象经验进行反思；最后综合两者提出设计启示。

Result: 发现"机器人操作"是一个涵盖高度复杂和异构活动的总括性术语，导致该劳动难以被表征和支持。

Conclusion: 需要从个体支持和服务生态系统两个层面，为机器人操作员设计更好的工具和流程。

Abstract: Robots are increasingly present in human spaces, such as for conducting deliveries in hospitals, interacting with visitors at museums, and stocking items in warehouses. To ensure the seamless integration of robots into these spaces, a new role in human-robot interaction is emerging - the robot wrangler, namely an individual who is responsible for setting up, overseeing, and troubleshooting the robot. To understand the needs of this stakeholder, we conducted a scoping review that uncovered a typology of robot wrangling across the research literature, and discovered that wrangling is an umbrella term that collapses a highly complex and heterogeneous space of activities, often rendering this labor difficult to characterize and support. To further clarify and understand robot wrangling, we then reflected on our own firsthand and imagined experiences as robot wranglers within our own respective domains. Guided by the scoping review and our reflections, we devise a series of design implications for supporting wranglers directly as individuals and as members of a wider service ecology.

</details>


### [176] [Dynamic Plasma Shape Control with Arbitrary Sensor Subsets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15935)
*D. Sorokin,M. Stokolesov,A. Granovskiy,I. Prokofyev,E. Adishchev,M. Nurgaliev,E. Khayrutdinov,G. Subbotin,R. Clark,D. Orlov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于强化学习的托卡马克等离子体形状控制方法，采用非对称Actor-Critic架构，通过端到端方法同时解决动态形状跟踪和传感器故障鲁棒性问题，在DIII-D装置上完成了实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统等离子体形状控制方法依赖平衡重构加线性控制器，且假设传感器完全正常，无法同时应对动态形状目标变化和传感器故障。本文旨在通过强化学习解决这两大局限性。

Method: 在NSFsim高保真模拟器中训练强化学习智能体；采用120个实验等离子体形状的数据集，每0.25s随机阶跃改变形状目标；训练时随机屏蔽30%的磁传感器；使用非对称Actor-Critic架构，Critic利用特权平衡信息，Actor添加辅助形状重构头。

Result: 在模拟器中静态形状跟踪误差为2.01 cm；动态轨迹跟踪在模拟和物理装置上定性验证；单一策略可鲁棒应对任意传感器子集；策略成功迁移到DIII-D实验和GSevolve模拟器。

Conclusion: 强化学习方法无需后备控制器或模式切换逻辑即可同时实现动态形状跟踪和传感器故障容错，且在真实托卡马克装置上完成了零样本迁移验证。

Abstract: Plasma shape control in tokamaks requires a real-time controller that tracks dynamically changing shape targets while tolerating diagnostic failures. Classical approaches decompose the problem into equilibrium reconstruction followed by a linear controller, and assume a fixed, fully operational sensor set. We present a reinforcement learning agent that addresses both limitations simultaneously. The agent is trained in NSFsim, a high-fidelity tokamak simulator configured for DIII-D, on a curated dataset of 120 experimental plasma shapes. The shape targets are resampled as random step changes every 0.25 s, exposing the agent to diverse transitions across the full shape envelope. At test time the agent zero-shot tracks dynamic shape sequences; on a held-out static configuration in simulation it achieves a mean shape error of 2.01 cm, and dynamic trajectory following is demonstrated qualitatively in simulation and on the physical device. Diagnostic dropout randomly masks 30% of magnetic sensors per episode, yielding a single policy robust to arbitrary sensor subsets without backup controllers or mode-switching logic. An asymmetric actor-critic architecture with privileged equilibrium information improves value estimation under partial observability; an auxiliary shape reconstruction head on the actor enables end-to-end shape reconstruction from raw diagnostics and serves as an interpretability tool for policy analysis. The policy transfers to experimental DIII-D shots, where it directly commands the coil actuators on two dynamic shape maneuvers, and to the independent GSevolve simulator.

</details>


### [177] [FocalPolicy: Frequency-Optimized Chunking and Locally Anchored Flow Matching for Coherent Visuomotor Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15944)
*Qian He,Zhenshuo Yang,Wenqi Liang,Chunhui Hao,Nicu Sebe,Jiandong Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FocalPolicy提出了一种结合频率优化分块与局部锚定流匹配的视觉运动策略，通过前景感知复合目标（时间域对齐+频率域正则化）和局部锚定采样技术，解决了现有方法中跨块动作不连贯的问题，在复杂操作任务中 outperforms 现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉运动策略生成的轨迹难以兼顾局部精度与长远规划，跨动作块之间存在不连续性，阻碍了长程连贯动作的学习。

Method: 1) 前景复合目标：对近端动作监督时域对齐，对多个未来动作块进行频域正则化以改善跨块连贯性；2) 局部锚定采样：在一致性流匹配训练中增强目标信号传播效率，从而高效学习复杂动作分布。

Result: FocalPolicy在复杂操作任务上优于现有方法，且其模块具有良好的通用性，可迁移至其他基线模型。

Conclusion: 通过频域约束与局部锚定流匹配，FocalPolicy实现了精度与远见的协同平衡，有效解决了跨块不连续问题。

Abstract: Visuomotor policies aim to learn complex manipulation tasks from expert demonstrations. However, generating smooth and coherent trajectories remains challenging, as it requires balancing proximal precision with distal foresight. Existing approaches typically focus on optimizing intra-chunk action distributions, often neglecting the inter-chunk coherence. Consequently, inter-chunk discontinuities significantly impede the learning of coherent long-horizon actions. To overcome this limitation and achieve a synergetic balance between precision and foresight, we propose FocalPolicy, a foresight-aware visuomotor policy that combines Frequency-Optimized Chunking with Locally Anchored flow matching. We introduce a foresight composite objective that supervises time-domain alignment within the proximal actions while regularizing frequency-domain structure over multiple future action chunks to improve cross-chunk coherence. To efficiently learn complex action distributions, we design locally anchored campling to enhance target signal propagation efficiency during consistency flow matching training. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FocalPolicy outperforms existing approaches and confirm the generalizability of our modules to other baselines. Project website: https://focalpolicy.github.io/

</details>


### [178] [OHP-RL: Online Human Preference as Guidance in Reinforcement Learning for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15971)
*Yunyang Mo,Jian Li,Qiwei Wu,Yihang Kang,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出OHP-RL框架，将人类干预视为偏好信息而非精确动作模仿，通过状态依赖的偏好门控机制自适应调节人类指导程度，在Franka机器人接触式操作任务上实现更优性能与更低人工干预成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将人类干预仅作为辅助训练信号，未能充分捕获干预中蕴含的关于何时及如何引导自主性的丰富信息。人类干预通常编码了在安全和任务约束下对行为的相对偏好，而非精确的动作指令。

Method: 提出OHP-RL框架，将人类干预视为偏好信息；引入状态依赖的偏好门控机制，自适应调节人类干预对策略学习的塑造程度，使智能体能从间歇性、不完美的人类反馈中获益，同时保持自主探索和稳定策略优化。

Result: 在Franka机器人三个真实世界接触式操作任务上，OHP-RL相比现有方法持续实现更高的成功率、更快的收敛速度以及显著更低的人工干预成本，且学习到的策略表现出更稳定、与人类更对齐的行为。

Conclusion: 将人类干预作为偏好信息而非精确动作模仿，并辅以自适应门控机制，能够有效提升强化学习在真实机器人操作任务中的样本效率、安全性和人机协作效果。

Abstract: While reinforcement learning (RL) enables robots to acquire skills autonomously, its real-world deployment is severely limited by inefficient and unsafe exploration. Human-in-the-loop interventions offer a practical solution, yet existing methods typically exploit these interventions as auxiliary training signals, without fully capturing the richer information they provide about when and how autonomy should be guided.
  Human interventions often encode relative preferences over behavior under safety and task constraints, rather than prescribing exact actions to imitate. Motivated by this perspective, we propose Online Human Preference as Guidance in Reinforcement Learning (OHP-RL), a framework that leverages human interventions as preference information to guide policy learning. OHP-RL introduces a state-dependent preference gate that adaptively regulates when and to what extent human interventions should shape policy learning. This design enables the agent to benefit from intermittent and imperfect human feedback while preserving autonomous exploration and stable policy optimization.
  We evaluate OHP-RL on three challenging real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks on a Franka robot. Across all tasks, OHP-RL consistently achieves strong success rates, faster convergence, and substantially lower human intervention effort than prior approaches. Moreover, the learned policies exhibit more stable and human-aligned behavior throughout training.

</details>


### [179] [Constrained MPC-Based Motion Planning for Morphing Quadrotors in Ultra-Narrow Passages under Limited Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15999)
*Harsh Modi,Xiao Liang,Minghui Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于变形四旋翼在极端受限环境下进行形态与轨迹联合规划的运动规划框架，其核心是一种平滑指数障碍物代价函数，结合基于2D LiDAR的非线性MPC，实现了在狭窄缝隙中的安全导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人工势场法在狭窄通道中会产生高代价，人为阻塞可通行路径；现有方法难以兼顾狭窄空间通行性与碰撞 avoidance，且缺乏适用于变形四旋翼的轻量化方案。

Method: 提出平滑指数障碍物代价函数（含代价缩减因子），避免硬激活阈值；将2D LiDAR测量直接嵌入acados-based非线性MPC框架，实现形态与轨迹联合优化。

Result: 仿真与实验表明该方法能成功穿越典型斥力代价函数无法通过的狭窄走廊，计算高效且安全。

Conclusion: 该代价函数具有通用性，不限于变形四旋翼，适用于任意移动机器人在紧束空间中的导航任务。

Abstract: This paper introduces a motion planning framework to plan morphology and trajectory for morphing quadrotors under extremely constrained environments. We develop a novel obstacle avoidance cost function for nonlinear model predictive control (MPC) that enables navigation through extremely narrow gaps under limited perception from a 2D LiDAR. Classical artificial potential field-based costs typically have a high cost in narrow passages, artificially blocking the navigable path. In contrast, we propose a smooth exponential obstacle cost that preserves low traversal cost within narrow gaps while maintaining strong collision avoidance behavior. The formulation avoids hard activation thresholds and introduces a cost reduction factor to reduce the cost within narrow passages. Direct use of 2D LiDAR measurements in MPC allows navigation around arbitrarily shaped obstacles. The method is embedded within an acados-based nonlinear MPC framework. Simulation and experimental results demonstrate successful traversal of narrow corridors where typical repulsive cost functions would fail. The approach provides a computationally efficient and practical solution for navigating through tight spaces while maintaining safety from the obstacles. While we are implementing the framework on the morphing quadrotors, the cost function formulation is general-purpose for any mobile robot application, and is not limited to the morphing quadrotors. The implementation code is available at \href{https://github.com/harshjmodi1996/morphocopter_mpc}{Github Repo} and a short video is available at \href{https://zh.engr.tamu.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/310/2026/03/MPC_MorphoCopter_video.mp4}{Video Link}.

</details>


### [180] [Fast Expanding Safe Circular Regions for Efficient Local Path Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16009)
*Scott Fredriksson,Akshit Saradagi,George Nikolakopoulos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对机器人局部导航问题，提出一种基于几何算法的局部导航方法，通过从LiDAR扫描中计算一系列向目标方向扩展的圆形区域来捕捉自由可导航空间，相比优化/学习方法计算更快、规划视野更长，并在ROS2中实现和仿真验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有局部导航方法（如动态窗口法、模型预测控制、控制屏障函数及机器学习方法）在简单环境中表现良好，但在复杂场景下基于优化或学习的过程容易失效，需要一种更快速、规划视野更长的方法。

Method: 提出基于几何算法的局部导航方法：从局部LiDAR扫描数据出发，计算一系列向目标方向扩展的圆形区域，利用这些圆形区域表征局部自由可导航空间，从而生成导航路径。

Result: 在ROS2框架中实现并在仿真环境中评估，结果表明该方法相比现有方法具有更快的计算速度和更长的规划视野。

Conclusion: 几何算法式的局部导航方法在复杂场景下能有效避免优化/学习方法的不足，提供更高效、更长远的导航能力。

Abstract: Local navigation is one of the fundamental problems in robot navigation, and numerous approaches have been proposed over the years, including methods such as the Dynamic Window Approach, Model Predictive Control, and more recently, Control Barrier Functions and machine learning based techniques. While these methods perform well in simple environments, many of them rely on optimization or learning based procedures that can struggle in more complex scenarios. In contrast, this article proposes a more geometric algorithmic approach that enables a local navigation method with faster computation times and longer planning horizons. The proposed method is based on the computation of a sequence of circular regions from a local LiDAR scan that expand in the direction of the goal and capture free local navigable space. The proposed method was implemented in the ROS2 framework and evaluated in a simulated environment.

</details>


### [181] [Health-Conditioned Vision-Language-Action Models for Malfunction-Aware Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16056)
*Hüseyin Arslan,Özgür Erkent*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种健康感知的VLA模型（Health-Conditioned VLA），通过在VLA-Adapter架构中注入健康投影器模块，使机器人能够根据关节运行角度和扭矩能力等健康状态信息，自适应地完成关节退化情况下的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型虽能处理任务执行中的检测、预防和恢复问题，但无法适应机器人的物理退化（如关节退化、执行器故障、夹爪无力等），这些在现实场景中普遍存在。

Method: 在VLA-Adapter架构中引入健康投影器（Health Projector）模块，接受包含机器人关节运行角度和扭矩能力的健康向量作为输入；在LIBERO环境上收集128个遥操作故障机器人数据用于训练。

Result: 轻量级的模型扩展使机器人能够成功适应不同配置的退化关节，而预训练的VLA-Adapter Libero-Spatial-Pro模型无法完成此类任务。

Conclusion: 通过轻量级健康感知模块的注入，可有效增强VLA模型在机器人物理退化场景下的鲁棒性和任务完成能力。

Abstract: Research on Vision Language Action (VLA) models has been increasing rapidly in recent years. Although some of them focus on detecting, preventing, and recovering from task failures, they usually don't deal with adapting to robot's physical failures. In real-life scenarios, most robots face physical degradations in various ways such as joint degradation, actuator failure, or weak gripper. We introduce malfunction-aware (health-conditioned) VLA that takes a health vector as an input that gives information about robots' joints' operation angle and torque capability, and adapts its predictions to complete the tasks with the degraded joints. To achieve this, we inject a Health Projector module to the VLA-Adapter architecture and train it on malfunction robot data we collected on the LIBERO environment [1]. We collect 128 teleoperated episodes on Libero-Spatial tasks. Our results show that, with a very lightweight addition, the model can learn to operate successfully with different configurations of degraded joints which the default pretrained VLA-Adapter's Libero-Spatial-Pro model cannot. The code and dataset will be available soon at https://github.com/h-arslan/health-aware-vla

</details>


### [182] [Beyond Collision Avoidance: Multi-Robot Yielding and Spatial Affordance in Emergency Evacuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16115)
*Ning Zhou,Edmund R. Hunt,Nikolai W. F. Bode*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过虚拟现实疏散实验(N=56)发现，移动服务机器人在紧急疏散中应采用主动让出空间（如隐藏至避难凹室）策略，而非原地冻结或最短路径优先，且环境可供性显著影响人类心理预期，人机交互需从轨迹优化转向语义感知导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人避让策略仅关注碰撞避免和宏观流量优化，忽视了环境可供性与人类空间期望，需弥合宏观理论与微观感知之间的鸿沟。

Method: 开展了基于游戏的虚拟疏散实验（N=56），在有无避难凹室的走廊中测试四种机器人避让策略（Hide、LineEscape、Freeze、ShortestPath），测量个体心理反应。

Result: 建立了明确的偏好层次：Hide > LineEscape > Freeze > ShortestPath；环境可供性调节认知期望——主动利用凹室增强心理舒适度，忽视明显凹室（如LineEscape）会触发期望违背，导致感知认知延迟显著增加；先前机器人交互经验有助于解码复杂社交意图。

Conclusion: 紧急疏散中人机安全交互须从纯轨迹优化转向语义感知导航，未来工作将扩展到机器人集群与人群的复杂交互场景。

Abstract: As mobile service robots increasingly coexist with pedestrians, ensuring passively safe behaviour during confined emergency evacuations is critical. Existing multi-robot yielding strategies often focus solely on collision avoidance and macroscopic flow optimisation, overlooking environmental affordances and human spatial expectations. To bridge the gap between macroscopic theory and micro-level perception, we conducted a game-based virtual evacuation experiment (N=56). We investigated individual psychological responses to four multi-robot yielding strategies (Hide, LineEscape, Freeze, ShortestPath) across confined corridors with and without refuge niches. Our results establish a robust preference hierarchy (Hide > LineEscape > Freeze > ShortestPath), demonstrating that proactive space-yielding significantly outperforms freezing and efficiency-first approaches. Crucially, we found that environmental affordances heavily shape cognitive expectations. Actively utilising available niches amplifies the psychological comfort of proactive yielding (Hide). Conversely, failing to use an obvious niche (e.g., executing LineEscape) may trigger Expectation Violation. This is reflected in a drastically increased perceived cognitive delay, despite objectively unimpeded trajectories. Furthermore, prior robot interaction experience helps users decode complex social intents. Ultimately, this research demonstrates that safe human-robot interaction during emergencies must evolve from pure trajectory optimisation to semantically aware navigation. Future work will extend this framework to investigate complex interactions between robot swarms and pedestrian crowds.

</details>


### [183] [DexJoCo: A Benchmark and Toolkit for Task-Oriented Dexterous Manipulation on MuJoCo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16257)
*Hanwen Wang,Weizhi Zhao,Xiangyu Wang,Siyuan Huang,He Lin,Boyuan Zheng,Rongtao Xu,Gang Wang,Yao Mu,He Wang,Lue Fan,Hongsheng Li,Zhaoxiang Zhang,Tieniu Tan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了DexJoCo，一个面向灵巧手任务导向操作的基准测试和工具包，包含11个功能导向任务、1.1K轨迹数据集，并在多种设置下评估了现代模型，揭示了当前灵巧操作策略的常见局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧操作基准缺乏能体现灵巧手相对于平行夹爪独特操作能力的任务，且缺乏全面的评估流程，因此需要建立更标准化的基准来系统评估灵巧手操作能力。

Method: 设计了11个功能导向任务评估工具使用、双手协调、长时程执行和推理能力；开发了低成本数据采集系统，收集了1.1K轨迹；支持域随机化评估鲁棒性；在视觉/动力学随机化、多任务训练、动作头适配等多样化设置下进行基准测试。

Result: 通过大量实证分析，识别出当前灵巧操作策略的几个重要洞察和常见局限，揭示了灵巧手机器人学习的未来研究关键挑战。

Conclusion: DexJoCo为灵巧手任务导向操作提供了标准化评估平台，有助于推动灵巧手操作能力的系统进步。

Abstract: Achieving human-level manipulation requires dexterous robotic hands capable of complex object interactions. Advancing such capabilities further demands standardized benchmarks for systematic evaluation. However, existing dexterous benchmarks lack tasks that reflect the unique manipulation capabilities of dexterous hands over parallel grippers, as well as comprehensive evaluation pipelines. In this paper, we present DexJoCo, a benchmark and toolkit for task-oriented dexterous manipulation, comprising 11 functionally grounded tasks that evaluate tool-use, bimanual coordination, long-horizon execution, and reasoning. We develop a low-cost data collection system and collect 1.1K trajectories across these tasks, with support for domain randomization to assess robustness. We benchmark modern models under diverse settings, including visual and dynamics randomization, multi-task training, and action-head adaptation. Through extensive empirical analysis, we identify several important insights and common limitations of current policies in dexterous manipulation, highlighting key challenges for future research in dexterous hand robot learning. Project page available at: https://dexjoco.github.io

</details>


### [184] [Learning Sim-Grounded Policies for Bimanual Rope Manipulation from Human Teleoperation Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16043)
*Gina Wigginghaus,Tim Missal,Berk Guler,Simon Manschitz,Jan Peters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究比较了基于视觉（手腕RGB摄像头）和基于状态（3D粒子模拟）两种策略在双手机器人解绳结任务中的表现，发现状态策略在预测初始抓取动作时L1误差降低30.8%，揭示了像素观测与物理一致状态之间的可观测性差距，表明状态空间选择对小样本模仿学习泛化至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变形线性物体（DLOs）如绳索和电缆在家庭和工业中广泛存在，但其无限维构型空间和频繁的自遮挡使得操作极具挑战。遥操作模仿学习虽可行，但受限于人力成本，因此观察空间的选择对从小数据集中实现泛化至关重要。本研究旨在探究基于视觉的策略在解绳结任务中泛化不足的根本原因是否在于观察空间本身，而非策略架构或数据规模。

Method: 在相同双手机器人遥操作数据上训练两个Action Chunking with Transformers策略：视觉策略使用两个手腕摄像头获得的自我中心RGB图像作为输入；状态策略使用通过多视角融合从初始观测提取并利用基于粒子的扩展Position-Based Dynamics（XPBD）仿真演化的DLO三维粒子状态。在未见过的绳索构型上进行开环评估。

Result: 在预测初始抓取和拉动动作时，基于状态的策略相比视觉策略L1误差降低了30.8%，量化了像素空间与物理一致状态空间之间的可观测性差距。

Conclusion: 状态空间的选择对DLO操作任务中小样本模仿学习的泛化能力具有决定性影响，使用物理一致的状态表示能够显著提升数据效率，为从有限人类演示中实现更高效的机器人学习提供了方向。

Abstract: Deformable Linear Objects (DLOs) such as ropes and cables are widely encountered in both household and industrial applications, yet remain challenging to manipulate due to their infinite-dimensional configuration space and frequent self-occlusion. Imitation learning from teleoperation offers a practical path to bimanual DLO manipulation, but its scalability is limited by human effort, making the choice of observation space critical for generalization from small datasets. In this study, we investigate whether the lack of generalization in egocentric visual policies for the knot-untangling task stems from the observation space itself, rather than from the policy architecture or data scale. We compare two Action Chunking with Transformers policies trained on the same bimanual teleoperation data: a vision-based policy conditioned on two egocentric RGB streams from wrist-mounted cameras, and a state-based policy conditioned on the DLO's 3D particle state, extracted from an initial observation via multi-view fusion and evolved in a particle-based eXtended Position-Based Dynamics simulation. Evaluated open-loop on an unseen rope configuration, the state-based policy outperforms its visual counterpart with a 30.8% reduction in L1 error when predicting the initial grasp-and-pull action, quantifying the observability gap between pixels and physics-consistent state, and pointing toward more data-efficient robot learning for the DLO manipulation task from limited human demonstrations.

</details>
